## 7. Part T — Strategy configuration

Inside this part, a reference to "section 1" to "section 7" without a document name means this part's own section 7.1 to 7.7.

Prefix T. Baseline: trading-system-prod-snapshot, branch `main`, commit `970aabf3c78d777d9d34549e021f7c8e756c7bdb`. A line reference without a path is to the file named in the same row or record.

### 7.1 Scope and method of this part

**Scope.** `strategies/schema.py` (`StrategyConfig`, its 41 fields, field and cross-field rules, docstrings, `validate_strategy`); `strategies/loader.py` (discovery, errors, `force_intraday_only`, enabled state, the `name` key, the S10 cross-check `_validate_scan_webhook_map`, `scan_strategy_errors`, caching); every non-test caller and direct reader of the strategy files (call site and what it reads; logic handed to its owner); the 16 files of `config/strategies/` against the schema, their own comments and the locked decisions S1 to S15; the strategy guide `docs/02_strategy_yaml_guide.md`; `config/scoring_weights.yaml` (loader, direct readers, the "single source" header); `config/strategy_direction_registry.yaml` (seed against state, the "auto-maintained" header, A16's `config/cron_registry.yaml` comment). STUDY concern 12 (section 13.2) is assigned here; A01 S-39 is A01's and appears only as a pointer, with A02's own part in T-01.

**Method.**
- Clone checked: `git rev-parse HEAD` = `970aabf…`, `git status --short` empty (checked at the start and again on 08-Nov-2026 before writing). Nothing in the clone was written, run or imported.
- Every statement was re-read at the baseline with `git show`, `git grep`, `git log`, `git log -S` and `git blame`. Own scripts outside the clone, run with `python3 -I`: `work/T/conf.py` (field list of `StrategyConfig` from the source with `ast`; the 16 files parsed with `yaml.safe_load` and a duplicate-key-detecting loader; keys, absent keys, encodings, line ends, name against file stem, LONG/SHORT pair comparison, file values against schema defaults); `work/T/urls.py` and `work/T/urls2.py` (shape of the scanner URLs only: host count, path pattern, equality between the two scanner files; no URL value copied); `work/T/lib.py` (synthetic inputs of no legacy origin, Python 3.13.18, PyYAML 6.0.3 = pinned, pydantic 2.13.5 against pinned 2.13.0): a non-UTF-8 file read through a text stream raises `UnicodeDecodeError`, which is a `ValueError` and not a `yaml.YAMLError`; the same bytes read as a binary stream raise PyYAML's `ReaderError`, which is a `YAMLError`; a top-level key that YAML reads as non-string (for example `on:`) makes `Model(**raw)` raise `TypeError: keywords must be strings` before pydantic runs; duplicate keys keep the last value; an empty document is `None`; `Path.glob("*.yaml")` matches dot-files and directories named `*.yaml` and not `*.yml`. Library behaviour is INTERPRETATION; the installed versions on the VM are UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED.
- Tests were read as documents only. Rama has made no statement to this project about A02 behaviour; statements that legacy records attribute to Rama are HISTORICAL.

**Intent sources and short names** (all HISTORICAL; dates are line dates by `git blame` unless a file date is given):
- LOCKED-YAML: `docs/locked_decisions.yaml` (file 2026-07-22); the section `strategies_module` S1 to S15 is L2600 to L2791, all lines 2026-04-17 (`bcf03b5`); the 22-Jul sweep (`245569a`) did not touch it. P9a L387 to L393, P9b L395 to L402, P17 L370 to L376, QS1 L2795 to L2804.
- M27: `docs/web_claude/01_module_instructions/module_27_strategies.txt` (2026-04-17), the build instruction that "DEFINES" the strategy schema and says "VS Code Claude must lock S1-S20 before building" (L6 to L8) and to record them "under strategies_module: section" (L305). INTERPRETATION applied throughout: LOCKED-YAML's S section is that locked record and names "MODULE 27" in its header (L2601), so where M27 and LOCKED-YAML differ, LOCKED-YAML is taken as the replacing source under the supersession rule of the A01 AUDIT, section 1.6; the difference is still recorded.
- GUIDE: `docs/02_strategy_yaml_guide.md` (one commit, `37e6698`, 2026-06-03).
- CFGGUIDE: `docs/CONFIG_GUIDE.md` (file 2026-07-28; the cited lines are dated in each record).
- AUDIT-05JUL: `docs/audit/audit_05jul2026.md` (file 2026-08-07). INTEGRITY-2026: `docs/audit/integrity_audit_2026.md` (file 2026-08-01). SMAP: `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` (A01 short name).
- Code comments, docstrings, log and message texts, file headers and commit messages, each with its date.

**Correction to the STUDY draft (applied in the STUDY before assembly).** STUDY section 6.3 says `orders/eod_squareoff.py` L695 to L696 reaches `build_direction_map` "through `canonical_direction`". At the baseline `eod_squareoff.py` L695 to L696 imports and calls `build_direction_map("config")` directly; `canonical_direction` (`core/strategy_direction.py` L74 to L79) has no non-test caller (FACT, `git grep`).

### 7.3 Issue and UNKNOWN records

##### T-01 — `validate_strategy` does not raise `ConfigSchemaError` on every failure (S8)

- **Area and flow:** strategy file parse and validation; every caller of `validate_strategy` and `StrategyLoader.load_all_strategies`.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Failure handling; Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** LOCKED-YAML S8 L2700 to L2708 (2026-04-17): `validate_strategy` "Raises ConfigSchemaError on any failure"; rationale "callers get ConfigSchemaError (not ValidationError) for uniformity". M27 S8 L145 to L149 (2026-04-17): "Raises ConfigSchemaError on failure". The function's docstring `strategies/schema.py` L351 (2026-04-17): "Raises ConfigSchemaError." The loader's docstring L111 ("ANY invalid file raises ConfigSchemaError immediately") and comment L135 ("validate_strategy raises ConfigSchemaError on any failure"). LOCKED-YAML S4 L2651 and S9 L2716 (unknown keys refused; "ANY invalid file -> ConfigSchemaError"). No source states that any failure escapes untyped.
- **Actual code path:** `strategies/schema.py::validate_strategy` L350 to L375: `open(yaml_path, "r", encoding="utf-8")` and `yaml.safe_load(fh)` (L352 to L354) inside a `try` whose handlers are `FileNotFoundError` (L355 to L358) and `yaml.YAMLError` (L359 to L362); the non-mapping check (L364 to L368); `StrategyConfig(**raw)` (L371) inside a `try` whose only handler is `ValidationError` (L372 to L375). Propagation: `strategies/loader.py` L136 (no handler), L85 to L87 (`scan_strategy_errors`, `except Exception`).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: missing file, YAML syntax error, non-mapping (including an empty file) and schema failure all become `ConfigSchemaError`. INTERPRETATION (Python and PyYAML semantics, confirmed on synthetic input): (i) a file that is not valid UTF-8 raises `UnicodeDecodeError` from the text stream inside `safe_load`; it is not a `YAMLError` and escapes raw (had the file been read as bytes, PyYAML would raise `ReaderError`, a `YAMLError`, which L359 converts, as the central loader does for the registry files by reading bytes); (ii) a top-level key that YAML reads as a non-string (for example `on`, `yes`, `no`, `off`, a number, a date or `null`) makes `StrategyConfig(**raw)` raise `TypeError: keywords must be strings` before pydantic runs; it is not a `ValidationError` and escapes raw; (iii) other `OSError`s (a permission error; a directory named `*.yaml`, which the glob at loader L128 matches) escape raw. FACT (files): all 16 files are valid UTF-8, have only string top-level keys and no BOM, so every sub-case is latent at the baseline. Consequences at the callers (FACT for each handler): `check_strategy_configs` (`utils/startup_checks.py` L1288), `scan_strategy_errors` (L86), the control tower (`ops/control_tower/aggregator.py` L159), pre-flight `config_sanity` (L41) and `config_integrity` (L156, L174), `build_direction_map` (`core/strategy_direction.py` L67), `build_taxonomy_map` (`strategies/taxonomy.py` L36) and `strategy_status` (L68) all catch `Exception`, so none depends on the type; `scripts/replay_signals.py` L187 to L188 and `main.py` L3159 to L3162 have no handler. INTERPRETATION (boot): for sub-case (ii) the boot still blocks with `invalid_strategy_configs`, exit 3 and the alert, but `_describe_config_error` falls back to `str(exc)` (loader L48 to L67) so the reason names no field ("keywords must be strings"); for sub-case (i) the boot ends with exit 2 before the alert (A01 S-39, A01's).
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `work/T/lib.py` (synthetic tests); `work/T/conf.py` (encoding, BOM and key types of the 16 files); `git grep` of callers and their `except` clauses. Tests (documents only): `tests/unit/test_strategies.py` (2026-07-18) has no non-UTF-8 or non-string-key case (`git grep` for "unicode", "utf", "encoding").
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. KNOWN DEFECT for sub-cases (i) and (ii), provisional until the review and the issue discussion. The four conditions of the A01 AUDIT section 1.6 hold for each: (a) the code has a mechanism for each case. For (i): the `try` around the read and the parse (L352 to L362) is the function's error normalisation, which turns a file that cannot be read or parsed into `ConfigSchemaError`; its `yaml.YAMLError` handler (L359 to L362) is written for a file whose content cannot be parsed, and a non-UTF-8 file is decoded inside that `try`, as part of the parse (`yaml.safe_load` reads the text stream, L353 to L354). For (ii): `extra="forbid"` (L50) refuses a top-level key that is not a field, and the `ValidationError` handler (L372 to L375) turns that refusal into `ConfigSchemaError`; a key that YAML reads as a non-string is such a key. In each case the handler is the mechanism for that input even though it does not catch the exception actually raised: condition (a) asks whether the code provides a mechanism for the case, and condition (b) whether it can act. A01 applied the rule in the same way in M-33 (a row handler written for row parse errors, which a short row escapes with `AttributeError`); (b) by the language semantics the handler can never catch the exception raised (`UnicodeDecodeError` is not a `YAMLError`; `TypeError` is raised before pydantic and is not a `ValidationError`), whenever the case occurs; (c) a file that is not UTF-8 and a top-level key that is not a field are malformed inputs that this validation exists to reject, on the documented set-up; (d) the outcome, an untyped exception instead of `ConfigSchemaError`, contradicts S8 (LOCKED-YAML and M27), the docstring L351 and the loader's L111 and L135, and no source supports it. Classified on the raise contract alone, as A01 M-33. Sub-case (iii): ISSUE / INTERPRETATION; KNOWN DEFECT considered and not given: (a), (b) and (d) hold, (c) fails (a permission problem or a directory named `*.yaml` is neither the committed input nor a malformed input that the validation exists to reject). Whether any sub-case occurred in production: UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED.
- **Severity or impact where established:** latent with the committed files. When it occurs: an untyped exception from `validate_strategy` and `load_all_strategies`; at boot, (ii) gives exit 3 with an alert whose reason names no field; (i) gives exit 2 with no alert (A01 S-39).
- **Owner:** A02 (`strategies/schema.py`). The boot outcome is A01's (S-39).
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12 (and item 5, error classes); A01 S-39 (pointer), M-33 (model of classification), S-53.
- **Rama:** not needed to classify.

##### T-02 — The S10 cross-check is never requested, while the map's header and the decisions say it runs at startup

- **Area and flow:** strategy load and the scanner-to-strategy map at startup.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** LOCKED-YAML S10 L2724 to L2733 (2026-04-17): "During load_all_strategies: if scan_webhook_map_path is provided, every strategy name referenced under scanners[*].strategy must exist ... Missing strategy -> ConfigMissingError"; rationale "Catches misconfigured scanner-to-strategy mappings at startup, not runtime". M27 S10 L162 to L166 and build step L248 (2026-04-17): "Loader validates at startup: every strategy_name in scan_webhook_map must have a corresponding YAML file"; "Cross-validate against scan_webhook_map at load time". P17 (b) and (d), LOCKED-YAML L370 to L376 (2026-04-17): at startup the map maps every scanner to a valid existing strategy YAML and has no duplicate scanner names, "CRITICAL on any failure". The map's header `config/scan_webhook_map.yaml` L3 to L6 (2026-04-17): "Validated at startup (P17, S10) ... Duplicate scanner names -> CRITICAL startup failure". `core/config_loader.py` L2344 (2026-04-17): "Locked: P17 (no duplicate scanner names; every name maps to a valid strategy YAML)". The loader's docstring L112: "Optionally cross-validates scan_webhook_map_path (S10)". HISTORICAL records of the gap: AUDIT-05JUL L446 ("[MED] S10 startup cross-check confirmed dead in production") and L440; INTEGRITY-2026 L61 to L63 ("S10 cross-validation: still unwired in production").
- **Actual code path:** `strategies/loader.py` L151 to L153 (the check runs only when `scan_webhook_map_path is not None`), `_validate_scan_webhook_map` L169 to L213 (forward only; returns silently when the root or `scanners` is not a mapping, L187 to L192). Callers (`git grep scan_webhook_map_path`): only `tests/unit/test_strategies.py` L364, L392 and `tests/unit/test_pb01_registration.py` L34. No production caller passes it: `main.py` L3159 to L3162, `utils/startup_checks.py` L1284, `ops/control_tower/aggregator.py` L156 to L158, pre-flight `config_sanity.py` L37 to L40 and `config_integrity.py` L155, L173, `scripts/replay_signals.py` L188. `ScanWebhookMapConfig` (`core/config_loader.py` L2374 to L2377) validates entry shape only; `load_all` has no cross-file rule. No other startup or pre-flight check compares the map with the strategy files (`git grep` of `scripts/preflight/`, `utils/startup_checks.py`, `scripts/preflight_scanner_check.py`).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: the cross-check never runs outside tests; no startup step checks map entries against strategy names, and none detects duplicate scanner keys. INTERPRETATION (PyYAML): a duplicate scanner key in the map keeps the last entry silently. FACT (runtime, A06's code): a signal whose map entry names a strategy that is not loaded is rejected per signal with `UNKNOWN_STRATEGY` (`signals/signal_processor.py` L948 to L953). Effective value: at the baseline files the check would pass (16 entries, each naming one of the 16 loaded names; `work/T/urls.py`, keys only).
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `git grep` of callers and of `scan_webhook_map` in non-test code; the two audit documents.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / FACT. KNOWN DEFECT considered and not given: (a) holds (the cross-check exists), (b) holds in the wired code (no caller passes the path, so the branch is never taken), (c) holds (a map entry for a deleted or renamed strategy file is a malformed input that the check exists to reject); (d) fails: LOCKED-YAML S10's decision text and the loader's docstring L112 make the check optional ("if ... provided"; "Optionally"), so not every source is contradicted. The header's and P17's duplicate-name rule has no mechanism at all (a requirement not implemented).
- **Severity or impact where established:** a scanner-to-strategy mismatch does not fail the start; it surfaces only at runtime as one rejected signal per firing. Not established at the baseline files (they agree).
- **Owner:** A02 (the loader, the map file and its header, the loader comment L2344). P17 (b) and (d) at startup: A01 (`utils/startup_checks.py`) and A06 (`scripts/preflight_scanner_check.py`), handoff.
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12 and item 14 (the header claim); A01 IS-4.05 (P17).
- **Rama:** not needed to classify; whether the check was meant to be wired bears on treatment only.

##### T-03 — A missing or empty strategy directory blocks the boot without the invalid-strategy alert

- **Area and flow:** boot strategy validation and the boot-abort alert.
- **Issue category:** Failure handling; Weakness.

For the empty-directory case itself: intended behaviour not established (no source names it). LOCKED-YAML S9 L2716 and the loader L129 to L132 make "no files" a failure (`ConfigSchemaError`), which the boot treats as blocking.
- **Actual code path:** `utils/startup_checks.py::check_strategy_configs` L1276 to L1280 (missing directory: CRITICAL log, error string) and L1282 to L1291 (empty directory: `ConfigSchemaError("No strategy YAML files found …")` caught); `run_all_startup_checks` L1615 to L1618; `main.py` L2484 to L2502; `strategies/loader.py::scan_strategy_errors` L83 (glob over the directory).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: both cases record the blocking `invalid_strategy_configs`; `scan_strategy_errors` returns `[]` (the glob yields nothing; INTERPRETATION that a missing directory globs to nothing, confirmed locally), so `if _bad_strats:` (L2497) is false and `_alert_invalid_strategy_configs` is not called; the boot logs CRITICAL "Startup checks failed" and returns 3 (L2485 to L2502). Effective value: the baseline directory holds 16 files, so latent.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `tests/unit/test_strategies.py` L519 (document: empty directory gives `ConfigSchemaError`).
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / INTERPRETATION. KNOWN DEFECT considered and not given: (a) and (b) hold for the alert path; (c) fails: no source says the alert covers a directory with no file to name.
- **Severity or impact where established:** exit 3 with a log line only; no Telegram or e-mail alert, the outcome the 18-Jul comment calls failing "QUIETLY".
- **Owner:** A02 (`scan_strategy_errors`); the abort path is A01's and the alert A14's.

##### T-05 — S9 "Strategies loaded ONCE at startup" against repeated loads

- **Area and flow:** strategy loading and caching in the live process and in other processes.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** LOCKED-YAML S9 L2720 (2026-04-17) and M27 S9 L160: "Strategies loaded ONCE at startup; no hot reload"; rationale "class encapsulates loaded state for get_strategy". The class docstring `strategies/loader.py` L93 to L94 (2026-04-17). AUDIT-05JUL L406 (2026-08-07) repeats "loaded once at boot".
- **Actual code path:** the loader keeps state per instance only (L97 to L98, L155); every caller builds its own `StrategyLoader`. In the live process: `utils/startup_checks.py` L1283 to L1284 (inside `run_all_startup_checks`, `main.py` L2451, without `force_intraday_only`), `main.py` L3158 to L3162 (with it), and at end of day `orders/eod_squareoff.py` L694 to L699 (`build_direction_map("config")`, which validates every file again, `core/strategy_direction.py` L52 to L71, with a working-directory-relative path, not `--config`). Other processes re-read the files: the control tower, pre-flight (three loads), `scripts/strategy_status.py`, `scripts/strategy_registry_officer.py` L230, `reports/daily_report.py` L1519, the raw readers (T-15).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: at least two validated loads at boot and a third validation of every file at the end-of-day summary, each reading the files as they are at that moment. FACT: at the baseline `force_intraday_only` changes only an INFO log line (L142 to L148), so the two boot loads return equal content from unchanged files. INTERPRETATION: a file edited between the loads is validated by the boot check in one state and loaded by `main.py` in another, and the end-of-day direction map reflects the file at that time, not the loaded set. "No hot reload" holds: the live dictionary is never refreshed.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `git grep` of `StrategyLoader(`, `validate_strategy(`, `build_direction_map(`.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / FACT (repeated loads) and INTERPRETATION (effect). A01 C11 already records the boot double load as an A01 ISSUE (parallel derivations) and hands "strategy loading with and without `force_intraday_only`" to A07; it is not duplicated here: A02's own part is the S9 statement and the loader's per-instance state.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none at the baseline files; the effect needs a file change during a session (UNKNOWN whether this happened).
- **Owner:** A02 (the loader and S9); A01 (boot call sites, C11); A12 (`eod_squareoff.py` L696 and its relative path).
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 items 12 and 13; A01 C11, S-30, S-53.
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### T-06 — Value constraints: the S4 percentage rule and the type-only fields

- **Area and flow:** strategy schema validation.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Weakness.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** LOCKED-YAML S4 L2662 (2026-04-17): "Percentage float fields: > 0, < 1 (expressed as decimal)"; M27 S4 L119 to L120: "All float fields that are percentages: > 0, < 1 (expressed as decimal: 0.01 = 1%, not 1.0 = 1%)"; rationale "Strict validation at load time prevents invalid configs from reaching runtime" (S4 L2663). Conflicting with S4 in the same register: S3 L2631 to L2635 gives defaults 0.0 for `entry_offset_pct`, `sl_pct` and `tgt_pct`. M27 S3 L99: times in "HH:MM" format; GUIDE L67: `min_score` "(0-100)". M27 test plan L277: "float fields negative -> validation error". For `max_concurrent_positions` (FIX-135, schema L127, 2026-05-31) the intended range is not established.
- **Actual code path:** `strategies/schema.py` field validators L215 to L296 and `_val_cross_fields` L300 to L345.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT (AST and reading): of the 14 `*_pct` fields, 5 follow S4 exactly, (0, 1): `sl_min_pct`, `sl_max_pct` (L215 to L220), `smart_tgt_trail_trigger_pct`, `smart_tgt_trail_step_pct`, `pullback_wait_tolerance_pct` (L236 to L242). The other 9 do not: `entry_offset_pct` allows 0 (L244 to L249); `min_adr_pct` and `max_spread_pct` allow 0 and any value of 1 or more (L251 to L256); `sl_pct` is bounded only when `sl_method` is `FIXED_PCT` (L303 to L312) and otherwise unconstrained (a negative value passes with `ATR`); `tgt_pct` must be above 0 only when `tgt_method` is `FIXED_PCT` and has no upper bound (L322 to L326); `sl_gap_buffer_pct` and the three `trailing_sl_*_pct` have no rule at all (L90, L106 to L108). Thirteen fields are type-only: `name`, `display_name`, `description`, `enabled`, `sl_gap_buffer_pct`, `smart_tgt_enabled`, the four `trailing_sl_*`, `pullback_wait_enabled`, `max_concurrent_positions` (0 or negative accepted), `v3_playbook`. `min_score` has no upper bound. INTERPRETATION: the time rule (L329 to L337) accepts "9:5" while its message says "expected HH:MM". Effective value: every file value is inside the bounds that exist; under S4's literal rule the file value `tgt_pct: 0.0` (all 16) and the defaults 60, 80 and 40 of the absent `trailing_sl_*` fields would be refused, which is the S3/S4 self-conflict.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `work/T/conf.py`.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / FACT. The conflict between S3's 0.0 defaults and S4's "> 0" rule is a conflict between sources of the same date that the code resolves one way (by not applying S4 to those fields).
- **Severity or impact where established:** none at the baseline file values; out-of-range values (for example `min_adr_pct: 1.5`, a negative `sl_gap_buffer_pct`, `max_concurrent_positions: 0`) would be accepted at load and reach the consumers.
- **Owner:** A02.
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12 (and item 7).
- **Rama:** not needed to classify.

##### T-07 — The `*_pct` unit convention differs inside the schema

- **Area and flow:** strategy schema and its consumers' units.
- **Issue category:** Consistency across components; Weakness.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** S4 and M27 S4 (as T-06): percentages "expressed as decimal: 0.01 = 1%, not 1.0 = 1%". Later, for single fields: `sl_gap_buffer_pct` comment `strategies/schema.py` L90 (2026-05-30, FIX-130): "extra SL buffer during 09:15-09:30 gap window (0=disabled)"; the four files' comment, for example `config/strategies/gap_go_long.yaml` L26: "widen SL 0.3% during 09:15-09:30 gap-risk window"; GUIDE L36 and L85 (value 0.3, unit not stated). `trailing_sl_*`: schema L104 (2026-05-31, FIX-132): "all thresholds in % of target distance"; `orders/breakeven_manager.py` L18 to L19 (BM7, same unit). No later source names S4 or replaces it for these fields.
- **Actual code path:** consumers: `signals/signal_processor.py` L1877 to L1883 (`factor = gap_buffer / 100.0`; A06); `orders/order_placer.py` L2190 to L2192 into `BreakevenManager.register_trade` (`orders/breakeven_manager.py` L123 to L145; A10, A11).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: `sl_gap_buffer_pct` is a percent number (0.3 means 0.3 %) and the `trailing_sl_*` fields are percents of target distance (defaults 60, 80, 40), while the other `*_pct` fields of the same files are decimals (for example `sl_pct: 0.012` means 1.2 %). No validator guards either convention. File value: `sl_gap_buffer_pct: 0.3` in `gap_fade_long`, `gap_fade_short`, `gap_go_long`, `gap_go_short`; the consumer applies 0.3 %, which agrees with the files' own comment. INTERPRETATION: a value written in S4's decimal convention (0.003 for 0.3 %) would give 0.003 % without any error. INTERPRETATION, for A06 to confirm: the buffer applies only between 09:15 and 09:30, while the global entry floor `trading_hours.entry_start` is 10:00 (file value, `config/system_config.yaml` L28), so at the baseline file values it may never act.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / FACT (the two conventions) and INTERPRETATION (effect). The code agrees with the field-level comments and differs from S4; a conflict between sources resolved one way by the code.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none established at the file values; a unit slip is silent.
- **Owner:** A02 (schema and files); consumers A06, A10, A11.
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12 and item 19 (unit conventions).
- **Rama:** not needed to classify.

##### T-08 — S5 ("every field declared in every YAML") is neither met nor enforced

- **Area and flow:** strategy files and schema defaults.

No reliance on Pydantic defaults for absent keys"; M27 S5 L123 to L128 ("NO conditional presence") and build step L252; the schema docstring `strategies/schema.py` L46 (2026-04-17): "YAMLs must include all of them"; 15 file headers L2 "Locked decisions: S1-S15". Conflicting later comments that rely on defaults for absent keys: schema L75 to L77 (2026-06-24: `enabled` "Default True (opt-in absent → enabled) so adding this field is a zero-behaviour change for every existing YAML") and L131 to L133 (2026-07-12: `v3_playbook` "DEFAULT False → the 15 existing strategies are byte-identical"). None of them names S5 or replaces it.
- **Actual code path:** `strategies/schema.py` L53 to L139 (28 fields with defaults; pydantic fills absent keys).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT (`work/T/conf.py`): every file omits 5 or 6 schema keys: the four `trailing_sl_*` in all 16, `v3_playbook` in 15, `sl_gap_buffer_pct` in 12; the defaults (False, 60.0, 80.0, 40.0, False, 0.0) apply. Nothing checks presence.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `work/T/conf.py`.

##### T-09 — S6 (positional strategies) against the three positional files

- **Area and flow:** the files `positional_momentum_long.yaml`, `positional_sector_rotation.yaml`, `positional_swing_long.yaml`.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** LOCKED-YAML S6 L2676 to L2688 (2026-04-17): `intent: DELIVERY`, `order_protocol: LIMIT_TRIPLE`, `sl_method: ATR`, `sl_atr_multiplier: 2.0`, `tgt_method: ATR`, `tgt_atr_multiplier: 3.0`, `smart_tgt_enabled: false`, `pullback_wait_enabled: false`, `entry_end_time: 14:30`; M27 S6 L130 to L135 and S13 L212 to L219 (same values; M27 S6 omits the ATR methods). Later sources: commit `97800b8` (2026-04-18, BL-16): `tgt_method` "ATR" to "RISK_REWARD" because the ATR target falls back to a zero distance; commit `c98a3fd` (2026-04-29): `sl_pct: 0.02` as the fallback; commit `ea8d90b` (2026-05-07): `sl_method` to FIXED_PCT "(ATR not implemented ...)"; the files' L21 (2026-05-07): "ATR not yet implemented; using FIXED_PCT until ATR data available". The files' own header L3 (2026-04-17): "ATR-based SL/TGT, no smart_tgt". The entry window is T-11.
- **Actual code path:** the three files; consumers `signals/signal_processor.py` L1801 to L1811 and L1913 to L1922 (ATR branches log "not yet implemented" and fall back; A06).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: `intent: DELIVERY`, `LIMIT_TRIPLE`, `sl_atr_multiplier: 2.0`, `tgt_atr_multiplier: 3.0`, `smart_tgt_enabled: false` and `pullback_wait_enabled: false` match S6; `sl_method: FIXED_PCT` (2 %), `tgt_method: RISK_REWARD` (1.5) and `entry_end_time: 15:00` differ. The target change is replaced by a later dated decision (BL-16); the stop-loss change is described by its own sources as a temporary substitute until ATR is implemented; the header L3 still says "ATR-based SL/TGT". FACT: nothing reads `sl_atr_multiplier` or `tgt_atr_multiplier` (T-14).
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `git log -S` of the three files.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / FACT. MATCH parts: intent, protocol, multipliers, smart target and pullback switches; MATCH with recorded conflict: `tgt_method` (BL-16, later dated); ISSUE parts: the ATR stop-loss intended by S6 and by the files' own L21 is not implemented, and the header L3 contradicts the body.
- **Severity or impact where established:** at the file values the positional strategies use a fixed 2 % stop and a 1.5 R target; whether they trade is decided by the strategy-control layers (A07) and at the file values (`trade_type: BOTH`, `force_intraday_only: false`, `delivery_enabled: true`) they are not dormant (INTERPRETATION from `strategies/control.py` L76 to L100).
- **Owner:** A02 (files and their headers); A06 (the ATR branches).
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12; STUDY section 15, question 6; T-11, T-12, T-14.
- **Rama:** needed: whether S6's ATR methods remain the intent for the positional strategies (STUDY question 6).

##### T-11 — Entry windows: the file values, their own comments and the other sources disagree

- **Area and flow:** per-strategy `entry_start_time` and `entry_end_time` and the global window.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Consistency across components.

History (`git show`): commit `38fa4e9` (2026-05-31, FIX-133 item 22) set per-strategy windows (gap_fade 09:15 to 09:45, gap_go to 11:00, positional to 14:00, others to 15:00), replacing earlier "TEMP PAPER ... REVERT BEFORE LIVE" values; commit `03ac3b0` (2026-05-31, FIX-134) "All strategy entry windows reverted to uniform 09:25-15:00 (Item 22 revert)". File comments: 7 files (gap_fade, gap_go, positional) `entry_end_time: "15:00"   # FIX-134: uniform window until paper testing complete`; 8 files "FIX-133 Item 22: default production window". `config/system_config.yaml` L28 to L39 (2026-07-16): "'09:25' is each strategy's INTENDED window; 10:00 is this temporary launch-phase floor". Schema defaults L137 to L138 (2026-06-29, commits `7103d74`, `931110c`): 10:00 / 15:00 "Matches trading_hours". `core/market_windows.py` L160 to L164 (2026-04-27, A01's file): defaults "09:30 / 13:30" and "gap_fade_long.yaml declare narrower cutoffs (e.g. 11:30)". GUIDE L23, L89, L103, L112: default 09:20, Gap Go 09:20, Range Breakout 09:45, Positional end 14:00. `pb01_breakout_retest.yaml` L69 to L71: its authoritative window is 09:20 to 11:00 in the watchlist stage, not this field (T-38).
- **Actual code path:** the 16 files; `core/market_windows.py::is_entry_allowed_for_strategy` L152 to L175 (A01) requires both the global and the per-strategy window.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: all 16 files declare start 09:25; 15 declare end 15:00 and pb01 11:00. INTERPRETATION (from A01's code at the file values `trading_hours.entry_start: "10:00"`, `entry_end: "15:00"`): the effective window of the 15 enabled strategies is 10:00 to 15:00. The start 09:25 is stated as intended by the latest source (16-Jul). The end 15:00 is described by 7 files' own comments as temporary "until paper testing complete", and no later source states the intended end for those strategies; the A01 comment and GUIDE describe values that no file holds.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `git show 38fa4e9`, `git show 03ac3b0`; `work/T/conf.py`.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / OPEN QUESTION (the intended end time for gap_fade, gap_go and the positional strategies) and FACT (comment mismatches).
- **Severity or impact where established:** at the file values every enabled strategy may enter until 15:00, including those whose earlier windows ended at 09:45, 11:00 or 14:00 to 14:30.
- **Owner:** A02 (files); A01 (`core/market_windows.py` comment, pointer).
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12; T-09; T-18.
- **Rama:** needed: whether the uniform 15:00 end was meant to be temporary, and which windows were intended for live.

##### T-12 — The schema accepts ATR methods that the consumer does not implement

- **Area and flow:** strategy schema against the signal processor's price derivation.
- **Issue category:** Consistency across components; Weakness.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** S3 and S4 (LOCKED-YAML L2632, L2634, L2654 to L2655, 2026-04-17) list `ATR` as a valid `sl_method` and `tgt_method`; S5 L2671 to L2672: "Fields not used by a method (e.g., sl_pct for ATR strategies) are set to their default placeholder values (0.0)"; S6: positional use ATR. Sources that ATR is not implemented: `signals/signal_processor.py` L1801 to L1811 and L1913 to L1922 (2026-04-17, "not yet implemented; falling back to FIXED_PCT"); its comment L1815 to L1824 (E.1, 2026-04-25): ATR strategies "declare sl_pct=0.0 (legitimately, since ATR computes the SL)" and the fallback then has "no usable SL distance"; commits `97800b8` (2026-04-18) and `ea8d90b` (2026-05-07).
- **Actual code path:** `strategies/schema.py` L35 to L36 (enums), L197 to L213, L303 to L326 (the `sl_pct` and `tgt_pct` rules apply only to `FIXED_PCT`).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: the schema accepts `sl_method: ATR` with `sl_pct: 0.0` and `tgt_method: ATR` with `tgt_pct: 0.0`, as S5 prescribes. INTERPRETATION from the consumer (A06 to confirm): such a strategy's signals are rejected (`ZERO_SL`, L1825 to L1835) or, for the target, fall back to a zero distance that the BL-16 guard rejects; with `signal_processor.atr_fallback_mode` set to `HALT` they are rejected as `REJECTED_NO_ATR_DATA` (the key is absent from the file and takes its code default; STUDY section 4.4). Effective value: no file uses ATR, so latent.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; commit messages.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / INTERPRETATION. The locked decisions S5 and S6 together describe a configuration that the schema accepts and the consumer cannot use.
- **Severity or impact where established:** latent at the baseline; not established beyond that.
- **Owner:** A02 (schema); A06 (consumer).
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12; T-09.
- **Rama:** needed for treatment together with T-09 (whether ATR remains intended); not needed to classify.

The only reader of the `trailing_sl_*` values (`orders/order_placer.py` L2174 to L2192) is behind `getattr(fill_entry, "strategy_obj", None) is not None`, while `_FillEntry.__slots__` (L498 to L503) has no `strategy_obj` (whether the guard can be true: OPEN QUESTION for A10 and A11). ##### T-15 — Three raw readers and one text scanner read the strategy files without the schema

- **Area and flow:** direct readers of `config/strategies/*.yaml`.
- **Issue category:** Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** LOCKED-YAML S8 rationale L2707 ("Thin public API"); S2 rationale L2621 ("simpler to glob and validate"); S1 to S15 name no other reader. Intended behaviour for readers outside the loader is not established.
- **Actual code path:** `reports/daily_report.py::_build_strategy_min_scores` L313 to L334 (`name`, `min_score` default 0; unsorted glob; `except Exception: pass`); `scripts/system_manager.py::_enabled_strategy_names` L502 to L519 (`enabled` default True, `name`); `ops_dashboard/backend/readers/config_reader.py::get_strategies` L56 to L89 (`name` or the file stem, `display_name`, `enabled` default True, `direction`, `intent`, `order_protocol`, `max_concurrent_positions` default 2, the two times, `tgt_risk_reward` with no default; catches only `OSError` and `yaml.YAMLError`); `utils/startup_checks.py::check_temp_config_values` L1465 to L1501 (raw text, "# TEMP" count).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: the readers apply no schema and keep their own defaults, which equal the schema defaults (`enabled` True L78, `min_score` 0 L116, `max_concurrent_positions` 2 L127). INTERPRETATION: at the baseline every key they read is declared in every file, so they agree with the validated values; for an invalid file they still report it (the dashboard as a strategy, the daily report with its `min_score`), while the loader refuses it; a decode error escapes the dashboard reader (STUDY 6.2 evidence) and the TEMP scan (A01 S-39).
- **Evidence:** the cited lines.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / INTERPRETATION (the schema is not the single path to the files; duplicated defaults). The readers' logic is handed off.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none at the baseline; divergence needs an invalid or incomplete file.
- **Owner:** A02 (schema and files, own part); A15 (daily report, system manager, dashboard) and A01 (TEMP scan), handoff.
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12; A01 S-39.
- **Rama:** not needed.

NO weights are hardcoded in screening/*.py — this file is the single source"; LOCKED-YAML P9b L395 to L402: "All weights, thresholds, and tier boundaries in config/scoring_weights.yaml. NO scattered constants in screener code"; QS1 L2801 to L2802; `core/config_loader.py` L2277 ("nothing hardcoded"); CFGGUIDE L329 to L331 ("No weights are hardcoded — this file is the only source"). For the signal-age step: P9a L389 (reject when older than 90 s) and the file's comment L20 ("≤ 90s").
- **Actual code path:** the loader and the live consumers (T-39, T-40); the copies and fallbacks: `scripts/v3_hardgate_parity_recompute.py` L41 to L46 (`_DEFAULT_WEIGHTS`, used when the read fails, L198 to L204, which opens the file without an explicit encoding, L201); `reports/daily_report.py` L141, L150 to L154 (`global_min = 60`; `open()` without an encoding; the same `try` also reads `broker_costs.yaml`); `screening/secondary_screener.py` L96 to L102 (`getattr` defaults for the v3 keys); `config/system_config.yaml` L608 to L611 (2026-07-12: `v3_chain.min_pass_score` 60, `medium_score_threshold` 65, `high_score_threshold` 80, "SEEDS"); `ops_dashboard/backend/readers/config_reader.py` L126 to L146 (fallback to `scoring.min_pass_score` in `system_config.yaml`, a key that does not exist there: `git grep` finds no `scoring:` section); `screening/step_executor.py` L406 to L423 (signal-age scores fixed at 30 s and 60 s).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: at the baseline file values every copy equals the file (weights 15, 10, 10, 10, 15, 10, 5, 5, 10, 10; thresholds 60, 65, 80; v3 defaults 50, 56, 75). FACT: the signal-age thresholds (score 1.0 up to 30 s, 0.5 up to 60 s, else 0) are constants in A07's code and differ from P9a and from the file's L20 comment (90 s); the file's L41 (2026-07-12) describes the old reject as "signal_age>60s". INTERPRETATION: a change to the file does not reach a copy when that copy is used (a failed read, or the dashboard's fallback); the two reads without an encoding depend on the process locale, and the file holds 51 non-ASCII bytes (A18's own comment, `scripts/forward_shadow_record.py` L48 to L51, records that risk for cron jobs); in the daily report a failed read also skips the broker-cost read in the same `try`.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `git grep scoring_weights`; `git blame`.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE / FACT. MATCH part (T-39, T-40): the live scorer path reads only the validated file. ISSUE part: the header's claim is not true of the whole system, and the L20 comment describes a threshold that the code does not use.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none at the baseline values; not established beyond that.
- **Owner:** A02 (the file, its header and comment, the loader); A07 (step constants, screener defaults), A15 (daily report, dashboard), A18 (parity script, `v3_chain` thresholds), handoff.
- **Cross-reference:** STUDY 13.2 item 12 and item 19; STUDY section 10.2 (duplicated score thresholds).
- **Rama:** not needed.

## 8. Part F — The configuration files, environment variables, sensitive material and the revert script

Inside this part, a reference to "section 1" to "section 7" without a document name means this part's own section 8.1 to 8.7.

### 8.1 Scope and method of this part

- **Scope (prefix F).** The 50 A02 configuration files as files (purpose against use; required, optional, example and unused files; file headers whose claims differ from the code); the directly read files and their readers' call-site handling (`security.yaml`, `preflight.yaml`, `expected_managers.yaml`, `index_universe.yaml`, `symbol_aliases.yaml`, the columns of `accounts.csv` as configuration, the reference-data CSV files); files expected but absent; configuration sources outside `config/`; environment variables; credential-related keys; sensitive material in tracked configuration files (locations only); and a static trace of `scripts/revert_temp_config.py`. Assigned STUDY concerns (section 13.2): 13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 20, 21. A01 handoff C12: the environment side only (which names exist where); the required-secret list and its exit codes stay A01's.
- **Baseline.** trading-system-prod-snapshot, branch `main`, commit `970aabf3c78d777d9d34549e021f7c8e756c7bdb`. `git rev-parse HEAD` returned it and `git status --short` was empty before and after the work. A line reference without a path is to the file named in the record's "Actual code path" field.
- **Method.** Every statement below was re-read at the baseline with `git show`, `git grep`, `git log -S`, `git blame` and `git show -s`. Own scripts, kept in the scratchpad `work/f/` folder and run with `python3 -I`, did the following on text copies only: an `ast` scan of every non-test Python file for environment reads, writes, `load_dotenv` calls and child-process `env=` arguments (281 files; 57 reads, 3 writes, 22 `load_dotenv` calls, 4 `env=` arguments in 2 files); a comparison of the `.env.example` block comments with the `accounts.csv` rows (flags and counts only); a comparison of the two scanner files (names, equality and host only); and a re-implementation of the revert script's two regular-expression steps on the baseline text (its table read with `ast.literal_eval`). The revert script, the tests and every other legacy file were never run or imported. Tests were read as documents.
- **Intent sources searched.** The files' own headers and comments; module docstrings, comments and messages; `docs/locked_decisions.yaml` (LOCKED-YAML, file 2026-07-22: P12 L333, P17 L370, CL1 to CL6 L730 to L790, AW2 L2055, S10 L2724, S14 L2771, S15 L2782); `docs/CONFIG_GUIDE.md` (2026-07-28); `docs/06_deployment_guide.md` (2026-06-18); `docs/temp_config_tracker.md` (2026-06-03); `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` (SMAP, 2026-09-02); `docs/SSOT_AUDIT.md` and `docs/SYSTEM_ASSUMPTIONS.md` (nothing on these items); `docs/foundation_engineering_rules.md` (FRULES, 2026-08-06); `docs/audit/` (in particular `system_security_audit_02jul2026.md`, SECAUDIT-02JUL; `c1_credential_exposure_inventory_02jul2026.md`, called here C1-INV, 2026-07-02; `ledger11_secrets_surface_measurement_04aug2026.md`, called here LEDGER11, 2026-08-04; `effect_verification_contract_01aug2026.md`; `registry_seed_state_split_10aug2026.md`); `docs/web_claude/01_module_instructions/` (M34 and others); `docs/web_claude/04_post_build_ops/task3_nse_reference_data.txt` (called here TASK3, 2026-04-17) and `docs/web_claude/03_audit_responses/response_54_audit_accounts_nse.txt` (called here R54, 2026-04-17); `deploy/hooks/secret_scan.py` (2026-07-02); the tests `tests/unit/test_revert_temp_config.py` (2026-07-25) and `tests/unit/test_fix133_multi_account.py`; commit messages. Every source is HISTORICAL. Rama has made no statement to this project about A02 behaviour; statements that legacy documents attribute to him are quoted as HISTORICAL.
- **Security.** No credential value, token, account identifier, e-mail address, IP address, fingerprint or absolute home path is reproduced. Per-account variable names are written `ZERODHA_API_KEY_<ACCOUNT_ID>`. Where a passage or a historical file holds such material, only its path, line or column is given. Comparisons of sensitive values were made by equality inside a script and only the boolean result was read.
- **Labels and rules.** As AUDIT_RULES.md and the A01 AUDIT, sections 1.5 and 1.6 (record fields, results, the KNOWN DEFECT rule). A finding inside another topic's code is a handoff (section 5) and is not counted here.

### 8.3 Issue and UNKNOWN records

##### F-01 — `slippage_model.yaml`'s header names consumers that do not exist and a use the code does not make

- **Area and flow:** configuration files as files; the paper slippage file of the central registry.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the header (`config/slippage_model.yaml` L3 to L5, `bcf03b5`, 2026-04-17): slippage "applied to paper mode fills and cost calculations (P12)"; "capital/cost_calculator.py reads this; symbol_validator.py assigns tiers". LOCKED-YAML P12 (L333 to L341): "Paper mode applies slippage to fills"; affects `capital/cost_calculator.py` and `paper/paper_engine.py`.
- **Actual code path:** `core/config_loader.py` L2445 (registration) and L2269 (`SlippageConfig`); the only consumer call site `main.py` L2512 to L2516 (inside `if is_paper:`, paper-only, not traced); `broker/slippage_engine.py` L4 to L20 (2026-06-16); `broker/cost_calculator.py` L32.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: neither `capital/cost_calculator.py` nor any `symbol_validator.py` exists in the tree (`git ls-files`); the cost calculator is `broker/cost_calculator.py`, whose docstring says it "Does not compute slippage" (L32). Tiers are assigned by `broker/slippage_engine.py` (SE2, L15 to L20) from the instrument cache's F&O flag. No live path reads the file's values (key map part 2: 4 keys, 0 live consumers). The header's "and cost calculations" describes no code.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `git grep` for `SlippageConfig`, `app_config.slippage` and `slippage_model` in non-test code (consumers: `main.py` L2515, `broker/slippage_engine.py`, the validator's registration and three presence lists).
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none on behaviour; the header misdirects a reader to non-existent files.
- **Owner:** A02 (the file text).
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 items 14 and 21; match F-29.
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### F-02 — `scan_webhook_map.yaml`'s header says the map is validated at startup; no startup path performs that validation

- **Area and flow:** configuration files as files; the scanner-to-strategy map at boot.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the header (`config/scan_webhook_map.yaml` L3 to L6, 2026-04-17): "Validated at startup (P17, S10): every strategy name must have a corresponding YAML file in config/strategies/. Duplicate scanner names -> CRITICAL startup failure." LOCKED-YAML P17 (L370 to L377): at startup "(b) scan_webhook_map.yaml maps every scanner to a valid existing strategy YAML ... (d) no duplicate scanner names ... CRITICAL on any failure"; S10 (L2724 to L2732): the cross-check during `load_all_strategies` "if scan_webhook_map_path is provided"; the loader's comment `core/config_loader.py` L2344 ("Locked: P17 (no duplicate scanner names; every name maps to a valid strategy YAML)").
- **Actual code path:** `core/config_loader.py` `ScannerEntry` L2347 to L2371 and `ScanWebhookMapConfig` L2374 to L2377; `strategies/loader.py` L152 to L153 (S10 only when a path is passed); `utils/startup_checks.py::check_scanner_connectivity` L652 to L700 (reachability only); `scripts/preflight_scanner_check.py` L197 to L231.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: the schema checks each entry's fields only; `strategies/loader.py` cross-checks the map only when `scan_webhook_map_path` is passed, and no non-test caller passes it (`git grep`; part T's item); `check_scanner_connectivity` and the pre-flight script test URL reachability only, and at boot the result is a warning (`scanner_unreachable`, A01 S-39). INTERPRETATION (PyYAML): a duplicate scanner key is accepted, the last value winning (part L, concern 6), so (d) cannot be detected after parsing. At the baseline file values the map has 16 distinct names and every mapped strategy has a strategy file (own script), so the gap has no effect today.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; own script on the baseline text (16 raw keys, 16 distinct; strategies equal the 16 files).
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT; INTERPRETATION for the duplicate-key case. Counted once, as T-02 (section 7.3), which records the same header claim with the S10 decision: merged at assembly; this block keeps the file-side detail.
- **Severity or impact where established:** latent: a map entry naming a missing strategy, or a duplicated scanner name, would not stop the boot; its effect downstream is A06's.
- **Owner:** A02 (the header; the S10 call is part T's, the duplicate-key semantics part L's); A06 and A01 for the checks they run.
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 items 12 and 14; part T (S10 never requested); part L (duplicate keys).
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### F-03 — `chartink_scanners.yaml`: a required file whose values cannot reach the check it is said to serve, with conflicting statements about whether its URLs are placeholders

- **Area and flow:** configuration files as files; the pre-flight scanner check (P17) and the boot's scanner step.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the header (`config/chartink_scanners.yaml` L3 to L5, L11 to L13, 2026-04-17): "Canonical Chartink scanner URLs used by the pre-flight health check (P17)"; "Note: URLs are placeholders. Replace with actual Chartink screener URLs"; LOCKED-YAML S15 (L2782 to L2791): "populated with placeholder URLs"; S14 (L2771 to L2780) gives `scan_webhook_map.yaml` its own `chartink_url` "for P17 pre-flight check"; the dashboard reader's docstring (`ops_dashboard/backend/readers/config_reader.py` L252 to L258, 2026-08-16): the URL in `scan_webhook_map.yaml` "IS REAL ... the production file carries the actual screener URLs". The conflict between S15 and the header (placeholders) and the dashboard comment (real) concerns identical values (below).
- **Actual code path:** `core/config_loader.py` L2448 (registration, required by `load_all`), `ChartinkScannersConfig` L2385 to L2388; `ScannerEntry.chartink_url` L2351 (a required field); `utils/startup_checks.py::_resolve_url` L764 to L776 (the map entry's URL first; this file only as a fallback); callers `main.py` L2459 and `scripts/preflight_scanner_check.py` L210.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: every validated map entry has a `chartink_url` (required, http or https), and `_resolve_url` returns it before the fallback (L767 to L768). INTERPRETATION (code chain): for any `AppConfig` that loaded, this file's values cannot reach the check; its only uses are the load itself, the hash, the W0 snapshot and the dashboard. FACT (own script): the two files hold the same 16 names and identical URL values, all on the Chartink host, none with a query part. So one source calls the values placeholders and another calls the same values real.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; own script (names equal, values equal, all map entries with a URL).
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT; INTERPRETATION for the unreachable fallback.
- **Severity or impact where established:** a missing or invalid `chartink_scanners.yaml` stops the live boot with exit 5 (`load_all`, `main.py` L2198 to L2201), although its values never reach a functional consumer. Whether the URLs are real placeholders or real screener addresses is not established from the repository.
- **Owner:** A02 (the file and its registration); A01 and A06 for the check.
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 14; F-18 (scanner URLs are not sensitive by any visible part); match F-46.
- **Rama:** not needed to classify; whether the file still serves a purpose is a treatment question.

##### F-04 — `expected_managers.yaml`: a stale header, and a file the live boot needs in effect although no list declares it

- **Area and flow:** configuration files as files; the effect-telemetry registry read at the end of the boot.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Failure handling (A02 part: the file's declaration and validation).
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the header (`config/expected_managers.yaml` L4 to L6 and L22 to L23, `a5c3704`, 2026-08-01): "Frozen 01-Aug-2026"; "where this file and the contract disagree, the contract wins until Phase B ships"; "Consumer: core/effect_telemetry (Phase B — NOT YET BUILT; until then this file is the frozen contract artifact)". The consumer's docstring (`core/effect_telemetry.py` L28 to L32, `132e571`, 2026-08-01): `assert_composition()` "live -> CRITICAL alert + continue (NEVER crash a live boot)". `docs/audit/effect_telemetry_phaseB_build_01aug2026.md` L49 to L50 (2026-08-02): "live CRITICAL+continue". No source states that the file is a boot requirement.
- **Actual code path:** `core/effect_telemetry.py` `_DEFAULT_REGISTRY_PATH` L63 (working-directory relative); `_load_registry` L129 to L142 (`read_text` at L138, no handler); `assert_composition` L152 to L216 (reads at L165); `main.py` L4243 to L4246 (catches only `EffectCompositionError`); the required-file lists (`core/config_loader.py` L2441 to L2450; `utils/startup_checks.py` L833 to L843; pre-flight `config_integrity.py` L16 to L24) do not list the file.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: Phase B was built the same day (`132e571`) and the file was revised on 2026-08-04 (`0197923`) without its header changing, so "NOT YET BUILT" and "until Phase B ships" are stale. FACT: the file has no schema; `_load_registry` takes `managers` as a list and every field through `str(e.get(...))`. INTERPRETATION (Python and PyYAML): a missing file raises `FileNotFoundError`, a malformed one a YAML error, a non-mapping top level an `AttributeError`; none is caught at L4243 to L4246, so the live boot ends there through the entry guard (exit 2, `main.py` L4291 to L4293), after the workers have started. The pre-flight parses the file only for YAML syntax (`ConfigYamlValidCheck`, alert-only).
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; `git log` of the file and module; `git grep` of the three required-file lists.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT for the stale header and the absence from every list; INTERPRETATION for the read-site outcome, which is handed to A15 and A01 (section 5). KNOWN DEFECT not given for A02's part, which is text and declaration only.
- **Severity or impact where established:** at the baseline the file is present and parses; the outcome of a missing or malformed file is the owners' to classify.
- **Owner:** A02 (the file, its header, its declaration); A15 (`core/effect_telemetry.py`); A01 (the call site and handler). The historical `ctor` and `yaml:NNN` references inside the entries are key-level (part K).
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 items 14 and 20; A01 S-39 (exit 2 for unexpected exceptions).
- **Rama:** not needed.

The Phase 1 header's "Nothing here blocks access" is true of the `security:` block only. FACT: the header's "Read DIRECTLY by scripts/security_monitor.py" also omits three further readers (the pre-flight security checks, `scripts/system_manager.py::security_check`, `scripts/approve_ssh_keys.py`); `copy_gate.py` and `request_copy.py` are named at L137 to L139.

##### F-07 — `security.yaml` is read without validation, by readers that treat a missing or misspelt key differently

- **Area and flow:** directly read files; the security watcher, the copy gate, the pre-flight and the system manager.
- **Issue category:** Weakness (missing validation); Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the header (L3 to L6): read directly, deliberately outside the strict loader; `scripts/security_monitor.py` section comment (L105, "defensive defaults if file/keys absent") and handler comment L179 ("never fail to run on a bad config"); `scripts/copy_gate.py` L65 and L96 (the same). The file's own warning (L113 to L115, 2026-07-26): "an empty allow-list makes check_authorized_keys skip the unexpected-key test entirely ... silently disabling the check is worse than a wrong value." LOCKED-YAML CL3 (L751 to L760) makes typo detection the reason for `extra="forbid"`, for the central loader's models only. No source states how a typo in this file should be handled.
- **Actual code path:** `scripts/security_monitor.py::SecConfig` L109 to L148 and `load` L151 to L183 (copies only listed names, L157 to L170, without type checks); `_default_watched_files` L213 to L224; `apply_operator_ssh_baseline` L186 to L210; `scripts/copy_gate.py::CopyConfig.load` L80 to L98; `scripts/preflight/checks/security.py` L36 to L41 and L65 to L83; `scripts/system_manager.py::security_check` L821 to L828.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: an unknown or misspelt key is ignored without a message, and a listed key takes whatever type YAML gives. INTERPRETATION: a misspelt `expected_key_fingerprint` would leave the default empty string, which the file's own comment (L113 to L115) says silently disables the unexpected-key test. FACT: every key is duplicated as a dataclass default; the default watched-file list has 8 entries against the file's 9 (it lacks the sshd hardening drop-in, L128). A missing file gives defaults in the watcher, the copy gate and the system manager (no warning in the last), and a FAILED result in the pre-flight. The committed fingerprint and count are replaced by an untracked operator override when that exists (L186 to L210). At the baseline every key in the file is a listed name (own comparison), so no key is ignored today.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines; comparison of the file's keys with the two readers' name lists.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT; INTERPRETATION for the misspelt-key consequence.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none at the baseline file values; the effective fingerprint on the VM depends on the untracked override (UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED).
- **Owner:** A02 (the file); A17 (the watcher, copy gate, approval tool), A20 (pre-flight), A15 (system manager).
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 20; F-18 (the fingerprint and paths in this file).
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### F-08 — `preflight.yaml`: "Editing this file CANNOT break the boot" does not hold for every edit, and its one value is not validated

- **Area and flow:** directly read files; the pre-flight funds floor, and the boot's text scan of every top-level YAML.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Weakness (missing validation).
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the header (`config/preflight.yaml` L1 to L13, `e45f6b2`, 2026-08-10): "NOT loaded by AppConfig"; "Editing this file CANNOT break the boot. A missing or unreadable file DEGRADES to the in-code default"; L36 to L37: alert-only. HISTORICAL, attributed to Rama in the file (L19 to L21) and the reader (`scripts/preflight/checks/broker.py` L33 to L37): the floor is Rs 2,000.
- **Actual code path:** `scripts/preflight/checks/broker.py` `MIN_BROKER_CASH_RS_DEFAULT` L41 and `_min_broker_cash_rs` L44 to L61; `utils/startup_checks.py::check_temp_config_values` L1465 to L1502 (top-level glob L1477, `read_text` L1481, `except OSError` only L1486), called at L1643 to L1644.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: not loaded by `load_all`; a missing file, a parse error or a non-numeric value gives 2000.0 (MATCH part). INTERPRETATION (Python): a file saved in a non-UTF-8 encoding raises `UnicodeDecodeError` (a `ValueError`) in the boot's step 14, which catches only `OSError`, and the live boot ends with exit 2 (A01 S-39); the same holds for every top-level YAML, so the claim is true for syntax and value edits but not for an encoding change. FACT: the value is accepted as any float; zero or a negative number is not refused. File value: 2000.
- **Evidence:** the cited lines.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT; INTERPRETATION for the encoding case.
- **Severity or impact where established:** at the baseline the file is UTF-8 and 2000; for the encoding case the boot outcome is A01's (S-39).
- **Owner:** A02 (the file and its header); A20 (the reader); A01 (step 14).
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 items 14 and 20; A01 S-39; match F-31.
- **Rama:** not needed.

- **Actual code path:** `core/config_loader.py::SmtpConfig` L884 to L949 (`_validate_password_source` L923 to L933; `resolved_password` L935 to L949).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: the schema requires one of `password` or `password_env`; no check refers to the mode, and `resolved_password` returns the plaintext field when `password_env` is empty. File values: `password` absent (default empty), `password_env` set (L459).
- **Evidence:** the cited lines.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none at the baseline file values.
- **Owner:** A02.
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 15; F-09; match F-45.
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### F-11 — `main.py --config` overrides the configuration directory only for some readers

- **Area and flow:** configuration sources outside `config/`: the command line; readers in the trading process.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual; Consistency across components.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** `main.py` L192 to L195 (help "Override config/ directory path"); M34 (`docs/web_claude/01_module_instructions/module_34_main.txt` L25, "--config PATH override config/ directory"; L420, a test that "--config <path> used"; 2026-04-17); IS-6.01 (M34 MAIN5). A contrary statement: `core/effect_telemetry.py` L61 to L62 (2026-08-01): the registry path is "relative to the repo root / process CWD, matching how the rest of the system resolves config/".
- **Actual code path:** `main.py` L2099 (`config_dir`), passed to `load_all`, the holiday guard, the notifier factory, `AccountRegistry.load`, the instrument cache, the startup checks and the strategy loader. Readers in the same process that build their own path: `signals/webhook_receiver.py` L265 (A06); `core/effect_telemetry.py` L63 (A15); `orders/eod_squareoff.py` L696 (`build_direction_map("config")`, A12); `core/strategy_direction.py` L43 to L44 defaults (A07); `core/account_registry.py::_read_primary_row` L308 to L310, from the module's location (A01; used by the primary-account tag and key helpers).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: with `--config X`, these readers still read the default `config/` (working-directory relative, or repository relative for `_read_primary_row`). INTERPRETATION: under the declared unit (`deploy/systemd/trading-system.service` L10, L18 to L19: no `--config`, the project directory as working directory) all paths name the same directory, so the gap is latent at the declared set-up. Scripts carry their own `--config` or `--config-dir` options with defaults that mix both bases (STUDY section 3.4).
- **Evidence:** the cited lines.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT; INTERPRETATION for the latency.
- **Severity or impact where established:** latent; with `--config` the symbol aliases, the expected-manager registry, the end-of-day direction map and the primary-account tag would come from a different directory than the rest of the configuration.
- **Owner:** A02 (the configuration-directory contract); A01 (the option and wiring); the readers' owners as named.
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 13.
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### F-14 — `.env.example`, the template for `.env`, omits 20 names that the code reads, among them names that scheduled processes require

- **Area and flow:** environment variables; the template against the reads.
- **Issue category:** Intended against actual.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** `.env.example` L4 ("Copy this file to .env and fill in real values", `7bc3367`, 2026-07-02) and L11 to L13 ("No ACCESS_TOKEN in this file"); SMAP L467 to L468 (`.env` holds the Zerodha secrets "incl. ZERODHA_USER_ID/ZERODHA_PASSWORD ... for headless TOTP login (FIX-187); GEMINI_BIN"; `.env.example` is the "Template for .env"); `system_config.yaml` L425 to L441 (`7bc073f`, 2026-07-25): the e-mail fallback is wanted ("`true` is the honest statement of INTENT") and "TO ACTIVATE: set ALERT_EMAIL_USER / _PASSWORD / _TO in the VM .env"; CONFIG_GUIDE L460 to L464 (`ALERT_SMTP_PASSWORD` is the password's source). HISTORICAL measurements: LEDGER11 §2 (2026-08-04): the VM `.env` had 24 keys, four of them not in the PC's (`ZERODHA_PASSWORD`, `ZERODHA_USER_ID`, `ALERT_SMTP_PASSWORD`, `GEMINI_BIN`), "required by design"; `docs/audit/audit_05jul2026.md` L170 (24 variables). `ops_dashboard/backend/config/gui_config.yaml` L80 to L84: `OPS_DASHBOARD_LOCAL_DEV` "is in no file" on purpose.
- **Actual code path:** the 57 reads (own AST scan; STUDY section 9.2). Readers that require an undeclared name: `scripts/auto_refresh_token.py` L349 to L353 with `_resolve_env` L97 to L108 (missing → CRITICAL alert, return 1, L359 to L367), scheduled at 08:15 Monday to Friday (`deploy/cron/trading-system.cron` L52); `core/config_loader.py::SmtpConfig.resolved_password` L935 to L949, called by `scripts/alert_watcher.py` L295 and L582 (a systemd service) and `scripts/monitoring_canary.py` L85 (`ValueError` when unset); `alerts/telegram_notifier.py::_send_email_fallback` L805 to L813 (warning, returns False).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT: the 20 names read and not declared are `ZERODHA_API_KEY`, `ZERODHA_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `ZERODHA_API_SECRET`, `ZERODHA_TOTP_SECRET`, `ZERODHA_USER_ID_<ACCOUNT_ID>`, `ZERODHA_USER_ID`, `ZERODHA_PASSWORD_<ACCOUNT_ID>`, `ZERODHA_PASSWORD`, `ALERT_SMTP_PASSWORD`, `ALERT_EMAIL_USER`, `ALERT_EMAIL_PASSWORD`, `ALERT_EMAIL_TO`, `TS_IGNORE_MARKET_WINDOW`, `TRADING_MODE`, `DB_PATH`, `GEMINI_BIN`, `TRADING_VM`, `CONTROL_URL`, `CONTROL_SECRET`, `OPS_DASHBOARD_LOCAL_DEV` (plus four supplied by the operating system). By kind: (a) required by scheduled processes: the broker user identifier and password (the token refresh) and `ALERT_SMTP_PASSWORD` (the alert watcher); (b) wanted but undeclared: the three `ALERT_EMAIL_*` names of an enabled block (`enabled: true`, L441); (c) generic broker names: `ZERODHA_ACCESS_TOKEN` is kept out of the template on purpose (L11 to L13) and, with `ZERODHA_API_KEY`, is written by `main.py` into its own environment only (F-44); in a separate process `scripts/reconcile_pnl.py` L99 to L100 (not scheduled, `scripts/eod_verify.py` L17) has no tracked source for them, and `reconcile_positions.py` and `refresh_instruments.py` use them only without `--account` (the scheduled lines pass `--account`); (d) operator and tool switches with defaults, one deliberately in no file. INTERPRETATION: (a) and (b) contradict the template's header, because a `.env` built only from the template would stop the token refresh and the alert watcher and leave the wanted fallback inert; (c) and (d) are consistent with their own sources.
- **Evidence:** own AST scan; the cited lines; `git grep` of the cron file.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT; INTERPRETATION for the effect of a template-built `.env`.
- **Severity or impact where established:** not established for production (the VM `.env` is gone). HISTORICAL: LEDGER11 §2 says the VM `.env` held the generic broker user-identifier and password names and `ALERT_SMTP_PASSWORD`, and not the three `ALERT_EMAIL_*` names.
- **Owner:** A02 (the template's key names); A04 (token refresh), A14 (alert watcher, fallback), A12 and A05 (scripts' fallbacks) for their reads.
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 15; A01 C12; F-26.
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### F-15 — `.env` reaches processes by three mechanisms with different parsing rules

- **Area and flow:** environment variables; how `.env` is loaded.
- **Issue category:** none (UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED, reclassified on 08-Nov-2026).
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the cron file's header (`deploy/cron/trading-system.cron` L7 to L9, FIX-189, 2026-09-10): "Python jobs source .env via `set -a && . ./.env && set +a` so secrets are EXPORTED"; CONFIG_GUIDE L497 to L500 (the same rule, enforced by a test); the systemd units' `EnvironmentFile=` lines; the scripts' comments, for example `scripts/fetch_daily_candles.py` L29 ("load .env so the api_key resolves under cron"); SMAP L470 to L472 (a systemd drop-in also injects the Telegram values). No source states which mechanism is authoritative or that a value must parse identically under each.
- **Actual code path:** 22 `load_dotenv` calls in 22 scripts, none with `override` (20 with the repository-root path, 2 with no argument: `scripts/refresh_instruments.py` L45, `scripts/zerodha_login.py` L36); `EnvironmentFile=` in four units (`deploy/systemd/trading-system.service` L13, `alert-watcher.service` L18, `security-watcher.service` L18, `trading-watchman.service` L13); the cron line in 42 jobs. `main.py` has no own call, but imports `scripts.zerodha_login`, whose import runs `load_dotenv()` (A01 C40).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT for the mechanisms. INTERPRETATION (systemd, bash and python-dotenv rules): the three parse quoting, `export`, comments and `$` differently, and without `override` the value already in the environment wins, so a process started by cron or systemd keeps the shell's or systemd's reading of each line; a scheduled script loads the same file twice.
- **Evidence:** own AST scan; the cited lines; `git grep` of `deploy/`.
- **Result and classification:** UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (reclassified on 08-Nov-2026 after the independent check, under section 1.6). A02's part, the variable names, shows no defect; the three loading mechanisms are A17's (the units), A16's (the cron lines) and A01's (the import-time load, C40) and are handed to them (section 5); no source states which mechanism is authoritative, and whether any value was read differently needs the `.env` file and the processes, which do not survive (F-26). FACT for the mechanisms; INTERPRETATION for the parsing difference.
- **Severity or impact where established:** not established.
- **Owner:** A02 (the variable names); A17 (systemd units and `.env` handling), A16 (cron lines), A01 (the import-time load, C40).
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 15; A01 C40.
- **Rama:** not needed.

##### F-20 — The revert script's "TEMP" test is a substring test: it matches the word "ATTEMPT" and the comment the script itself writes

- **Area and flow:** the temporary-configuration mechanism; detection in `scripts/revert_temp_config.py`.
- **Issue category:** Logic and calculation; Intended against actual.
- **Intended behaviour and source:** the docstring (L4 to L6, `37e6698`, 2026-06-03): "Scan config YAML files for TEMP markers"; exit 0 for "no TEMP values found (clean)" (L13); the clean message "All TEMP values have been reverted. Config is production-ready." (L214); the instruction after an apply, "Re-scan to verify" (L227 to L228); `docs/temp_config_tracker.md` L45 to L48 (2026-06-03): "REVERTED: Production value restored, TEMP marker removed"; the markers then in the files were comments "# TEMP: ..." (for example `37e6698:config/system_config.yaml` L99 to L100); the test `test_dry_run_clean` (`tests/unit/test_revert_temp_config.py` L114 to L118): a file without markers returns 0 and "production-ready". No source treats any other word containing the letters TEMP as a marker.
- **Actual code path:** `scan_temp_markers` L116 to L144 (test `"TEMP" in content and re.search(pattern, content)`, L125); `apply_reverts` L147 to L174 (writes `# REVERTED from TEMP <temp_value>`, L162).
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT (own re-implementation on the baseline text): `system_config.yaml` contains "TEMP" only inside "ATTEMPT" (L407, added by `488e7e3`, 2026-07-25) and no "# TEMP" marker, so all six `system_config.yaml` entries are classified ACTIVE_TEMP; a dry run reports six active TEMP values and returns 2. (ii) After any apply the file contains the script's own text "REVERTED from TEMP", and every pattern still matches, so the prescribed re-scan classifies every entry ACTIVE again and can never return 0; this holds since the first version.
- **Evidence:** own script; `git log -S ATTEMPT`; the test read as a document.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE; KNOWN DEFECT for both parts, provisional until the review and the issue discussion. The four conditions of the A01 AUDIT, section 1.6 hold: (a) the code has a mechanism for the case, the marker test at L125 that sorts entries into ACTIVE_TEMP and ALREADY_REVERTED; (b) by Python's semantics `"TEMP" in content` is true for certain whenever the text contains "ATTEMPT" or the script's own "REVERTED from TEMP"; (c) a file without TEMP markers, and the re-scan after an apply, are cases the code says it handles (L13, L214, L227 to L228), and both arise under the documented set-up: the committed `system_config.yaml` and the documented commands (the tracker L29 to L35, the deployment guide L240); (d) the outcome (active entries reported, exit 2, and with `--apply --confirm` six lines rewritten) contradicts every intent source found. Whether it occurred: UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (F-25).
- **Severity or impact where established:** at the baseline a dry run is misleading; with `--apply --confirm` the consequences are F-22 and F-24.
- **Owner:** A02.
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 16; A01 S-56 (the startup check counts the different marker "# TEMP").
- **Rama:** not needed to classify.

- **Actual code path:** `TEMP_REVERTS` patterns (for example L49 `daily_loss_limit_pct:\s*[\d.]+`); `re.sub(..., count=1, flags=re.MULTILINE)` L160 to L166; `key_name` from the pattern L157 to L158.
- **Actual behaviour and effective value:** FACT (own re-implementation): the `daily_loss_limit_pct` pattern matches two lines at the baseline, L316 (`risk.daily_loss_limit_pct`) and L317 (`delivery_daily_loss_limit_pct`, as a substring); the first match is replaced, which is L316 only because of file order. The dotted key is a label used in messages only (L170 to L171, L189); the written key is the bare name from the pattern (L157 to L158). INTERPRETATION (Python `re`): `\s*` also matches line breaks.
- **Evidence:** own script; the cited lines.
- **Result and classification:** ISSUE. FACT; INTERPRETATION for the line-break case.
- **Severity or impact where established:** none at the baseline file order; a reordering of the two `risk` lines would make an apply change the delivery twin instead.
- **Owner:** A02.
- **Cross-reference:** 13.2 item 16.
- **Rama:** not needed.

- **Area and flow:** the temporary-configuration mechanism; the daily loss limit and the `gap_fade_long` score floor.

- **Intended behaviour and source:** for the table values: `TEMP_REVERTS` L46 to L54 (`prod_value` 0.05, "(5%)") and L91 to L99 (60), the tracker L18 and L23, and the deployment guide L237 to L243 ("Switching Paper → Live ... 2. Revert TEMP config values: ... --apply --confirm"), all 2026-06-03. Later and against them: commit `b27f767` (2026-06-14): "daily_loss_limit_pct 0.05 → 0.03"; commit `c69f1fd` (2026-06-24), HISTORICAL and attributed to Rama: "Implements the 12 resolved config conflicts ... (all LOCKED by Rama). #1 SINGLE DAILY-LOSS SOURCE ... daily_loss_limit_pct (3%) is now the SOLE authority" (the same commit edited this script and kept 0.05); the file comment L316 ("SOLE daily-loss authority (3% of capital), PERMANENT"); CONFIG_GUIDE L38 (3%, "as of 2026-06-19"). For `min_score`: the marker comment at `37e6698:config/strategies/gap_fade_long.yaml` L36 ("lowered for paper testing (was 0 -> uses scorer default)"), against the table's 60 of the same date.
- **Actual code path:** `TEMP_REVERTS` L33 to L100; `apply_reverts` L147 to L174.
